gdp_view_on_action_fc = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			gdp_var > 5000000
			or = {
				capital_gdp_ratio < 0.5
				root.num_states <3
			}
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = gdp_view_fc
			value = 5
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			gdp_var > 10000000
			or = {
				capital_gdp_ratio < 0.5
				root.num_states <3
			}

central_bank_modifier = {
	# Modifiers read country variables that history/global sets after history/buildings: during the history
	# call (initialize_historic_macro_facilities_bc) they are empty and the multipliers evaluate in an empty
	# scope. The first monthly pulse (update_modifiers_bc_fc_ns) applies them.
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = base_demande_currency_fix }
		if = {
			limit = { exists = capital }
			if = {
				limit = { exists = capital }
				capital = {
					save_scope_as = capital_scope
				}
			}
		}
		every_scope_state = { #historic_place jamais netoyer
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_modifier = central_bank_place
				}
				remove_modifier = central_bank_place
			}
		}
		scope:capital_scope ?= { #actualisation capital
			#place
			if = {
				limit = {
					not = {
						has_modifier = central_bank_place
					}
				}
				add_modifier = {
					name = central_bank_place
				}
			}
			#historic_place
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_modifier = central_bank_place
				}
				if = { limit = { has_modifier = central_bank_historic_place } remove_modifier = central_bank_historic_place }
				add_modifier = {
					name = central_bank_historic_place
				}
			}
		}
		if = { #country - une seul fois
			limit = {
				has_modifier = has_central_bank
			}
			if = { limit = { has_modifier = has_central_bank } remove_modifier = has_central_bank }
			add_modifier = {
				name = has_central_bank
			}
			if = { limit = { has_modifier = central_bank_metal_purchases } remove_modifier = central_bank_metal_purchases }
			add_modifier = {
				name = central_bank_metal_purchases
				multiplier = central_bank_metal_purchases
				months = 2
			}
		}
		# if = { #country_already_central_bank -> defini avant notif joueur quand le pays a vraiment une bc
		# 	limit = {
		# 		# any_scope_state = {
		# 		# 	has_building = building_bank
		# 		# }
		# 		has_building = building_bank
		# 		not = {
		# 			has_variable = country_already_central_bank
		# 		}
		# 	}
		# 	set_variable = { name = country_already_central_bank }
		# }

		# the CB's fixed demand variables (E&F set them from its building; the building's currency_demande modifier had no
		# output to move -- Д.R9б.1)
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_modifier = has_central_bank
				any_scope_state = { has_modifier = central_bank_place }
			}
			central_bank_modifier_fixed_var = yes
		}
	}
}   
   
financial_center_modifier = {
	# root is empty during history/buildings; the country itself is saved for the fixed-var calls.
	save_scope_as = financial_center_country
    every_scope_state = {
        if = {
            limit = {
                has_modifier = financial_center_place
            }
            remove_modifier = financial_center_place
        }
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_modifier = financial_center_place_spe
			}
			remove_modifier = financial_center_place_spe
		}
        if = {
            limit = {
                has_modifier = financial_center_historic_place
            }
            remove_modifier = financial_center_historic_place
        }
		set_variable = {
			name = financial_center_site_var
			value = 0
		}
    }

	#reg
	capital = {
        if = {
            limit = {
				building_financial_centre_spe = no
				has_building = building_financial_centre
            }
			if = {
				limit = {
					not = {
						has_modifier = financial_center_place
					}
				}
				add_modifier = {
					name = financial_center_place
					multiplier = var:gdp_view_fc
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					and = {
						has_modifier = financial_center_place
						not = {
							has_modifier = financial_center_historic_place
						}
					}
				}
				add_modifier = {
					name = financial_center_historic_place
				}
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = financial_center_site_var
				value = 1
			}
		}
    }
	#spe
	every_scope_state = {
        if = {
			limit = {
				building_financial_centre_spe = yes
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_modifier = financial_center_place_spe
				}
				remove_modifier = financial_center_place_spe
			}
			# the multiplier reads var:gdp_view_fc in the state scope; the variable lives on the country
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:financial_center_country = { has_variable = gdp_view_fc }
				}
				set_variable = {
					name = gdp_view_fc
					value = scope:financial_center_country.var:gdp_view_fc
				}
				# max_building_financial_center_by_number_in_market counts the country's centres (any_scope_state): country scope only
				set_variable = {
					name = gdp_view_fc_by_num
					value = scope:financial_center_country.max_building_financial_center_by_number_in_market
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_variable = gdp_view_fc_by_num exists = scope:financial_center_country }
				# the multiplier is not evaluated in the state's scope (hotfix README, EF.36); a local variable is (PSC does so)
				set_local_variable = {
					name = fc_place_spe_mult
					value = scope:financial_center_country.max_building_financial_center_by_number_in_market
				}
				add_modifier = {
					name = financial_center_place_spe
					multiplier = local_var:fc_place_spe_mult
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					and = {
						has_modifier = financial_center_place_spe
						not = {
							has_modifier = financial_center_historic_place
						}
					}
				}
				add_modifier = {
					name = financial_center_historic_place
				}
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = financial_center_site_var
				value = 1
			}
		}
    }
	#Leader Stock Exchange
	every_scope_state = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_modifier = financial_center_place_main_stock_exchange
			}
			remove_modifier = financial_center_place_main_stock_exchange
		}
        if = {
			limit = {
				building_financial_centre_any = yes
				or = {
					or = { #pour les pays avec des FC historique qui ne disparaisse pas et qui n'ont pas de Bankl
						# the CB's state -- the capital of a country with its CB (Д.R9б.1)
						AND = {
							is_capital = yes
							owner = { has_modifier = has_central_bank }
						}
						owner = { is_revolutionary = yes }
					}
					has_building = building_financial_centre_usa
					has_building = building_financial_centre_chi
					has_building = building_financial_centre_bic
					has_building = building_financial_centre_jap
					has_building = building_financial_centre_sar
					has_building = building_financial_centre_que
					#has_building = building_financial_centre_frm
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_modifier = financial_center_place_main_stock_exchange
				}
				remove_modifier = financial_center_place_main_stock_exchange
			}
			add_modifier = {
				name = financial_center_place_main_stock_exchange
			}
		}
	}
	#####################################################################################################################################################
	# boucle pour les modificateur

	if = { #country
		limit = {
			building_financial_centre_any = yes
		}
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = has_financial_center } remove_modifier = has_financial_center }
        add_modifier = {
            name = has_financial_center
			multiplier = value_for_has_financial_center
        }
    }


    every_scope_state = {
		limit = {
			has_modifier = financial_center_place_main_stock_exchange
		}

		#building - # supply demand for financial product
		random_scope_building = {
			limit = {
				is_building_group = bg_financial_centre
			}
			save_scope_as = financial_center_scope

			scope:financial_center_country = {
				financial_center_modifier_fixed_var = yes
			}

			if = { limit = { has_modifier = bond_deemande } remove_modifier = bond_deemande }
			add_modifier = {
				name = bond_deemande
				multiplier = target_demand_bond_for_modifier
			}
			if = { limit = { has_modifier = manufacture_stock_deemande } remove_modifier = manufacture_stock_deemande }
			add_modifier = {
				name = manufacture_stock_deemande
				multiplier = target_demand_manufacture_stock_for_modifier
			}
			if = { limit = { has_modifier = agricultural_stock_deemande } remove_modifier = agricultural_stock_deemande }
			add_modifier = {
				name = agricultural_stock_deemande
				multiplier = target_demand_agricultural_stock_for_modifier
			}
			if = { limit = { has_modifier = mining_stock_deemande } remove_modifier = mining_stock_deemande }
			add_modifier = {
				name = mining_stock_deemande
				multiplier = target_demand_mining_stock_for_modifier
			}
			if = { limit = { has_modifier = railroad_stock_deemande } remove_modifier = railroad_stock_deemande }
			add_modifier = {
				name = railroad_stock_deemande
				multiplier = target_demand_railroad_stock_for_modifier
			}

			if = { limit = { has_modifier = mutual_fund_supply } remove_modifier = mutual_fund_supply }
			add_modifier = {
				name = mutual_fund_supply
				multiplier = target_supply_mutual_fund_for_modifier
			}

			if = {
				limit = {
					occupancy < 0.75
					not = {
						owner = {
							is_player = yes
						}
					}
				}
				set_subsidized = yes
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					occupancy > 0.75
					not = {
						owner = {
							is_player = yes
						}
					}
				}
				set_subsidized = no
			}
		}
	}
    every_scope_state = {
		limit = {
			or = {
				has_modifier = financial_center_place_spe
				has_modifier = financial_center_place
			}
			not = {
				has_modifier = financial_center_place_main_stock_exchange
			}
		}
		random_scope_building = {
			limit = {
				is_building_group = bg_financial_centre
			}
			save_scope_as = financial_center_scope

			scope:financial_center_country = {
				financial_center_modifier_fixed_var = yes
			}

			if = { limit = { has_modifier = bond_deemande } remove_modifier = bond_deemande }
			add_modifier = {
				name = bond_deemande
				multiplier = target_demand_bond_for_modifier
			}
			if = { limit = { has_modifier = manufacture_stock_deemande } remove_modifier = manufacture_stock_deemande }
			add_modifier = {
				name = manufacture_stock_deemande
				multiplier = target_demand_manufacture_stock_for_modifier
			}
			if = { limit = { has_modifier = agricultural_stock_deemande } remove_modifier = agricultural_stock_deemande }
			add_modifier = {
				name = agricultural_stock_deemande
				multiplier = target_demand_agricultural_stock_for_modifier
			}
			if = { limit = { has_modifier = mining_stock_deemande } remove_modifier = mining_stock_deemande }
			add_modifier = {
				name = mining_stock_deemande
				multiplier = target_demand_mining_stock_for_modifier
			}
			if = { limit = { has_modifier = railroad_stock_deemande } remove_modifier = railroad_stock_deemande }
			add_modifier = {
				name = railroad_stock_deemande
				multiplier = target_demand_railroad_stock_for_modifier
			}

			if = { limit = { has_modifier = mutual_fund_supply } remove_modifier = mutual_fund_supply }
			add_modifier = {
				name = mutual_fund_supply
				multiplier = target_supply_mutual_fund_for_modifier
			}

			if = {
				limit = {
					occupancy < 0.75
					not = {
						owner = {
							is_player = yes
						}
					}
				}
				set_subsidized = yes
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					occupancy > 0.75
					not = {
						owner = {
							is_player = yes
						}
					}
				}
				set_subsidized = no
			}
		}
	}

	#####################################################################################################################################################
	# ban good
	######################
	#bond
	if = {
		limit = { #tech à ajouter ici permet de délock l'import de pf étrangé au dela de 25%
			market.mg:bond.market_goods_imports > bond_market_goods_sell_orders_ratio
		}
		add_banned_goods = g:bond
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			market.mg:bond.market_goods_imports < bond_market_goods_sell_orders_ratio
			not = {
				has_modifier = speculative_share_modifier_1
			}
		}
		remove_banned_goods = g:bond
	}

	######################
	#manufacture_stock
	if = {
		limit = { #tech à ajouter ici permet de délock l'import de pf étrangé au dela de 25%
			market.mg:manufacture_stock.market_goods_imports > manufacture_stock_market_goods_sell_orders_ratio
		}
		add_banned_goods = g:manufacture_stock
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			market.mg:manufacture_stock.market_goods_imports < manufacture_stock_market_goods_sell_orders_ratio
			not = {
				has_modifier = speculative_share_modifier_1
			}
		}
		remove_banned_goods = g:manufacture_stock
	}

	######################
	#manufacture_stock
	if = {
		limit = { #tech à ajouter ici permet de délock l'import de pf étrangé au dela de 25%
			market.mg:manufacture_stock.market_goods_imports > manufacture_stock_market_goods_sell_orders_ratio
		}
		add_banned_goods = g:manufacture_stock
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			market.mg:manufacture_stock.market_goods_imports < manufacture_stock_market_goods_sell_orders_ratio
			not = {
				has_modifier = speculative_share_modifier_1
			}
		}
		remove_banned_goods = g:manufacture_stock
	}
	######################
	######################
	#agricultural_stock
	if = {
		limit = { #tech à ajouter ici permet de délock l'import de pf étrangé au dela de 25%
			market.mg:agricultural_stock.market_goods_imports > agricultural_stock_market_goods_sell_orders_ratio
		}
		add_banned_goods = g:agricultural_stock
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			market.mg:agricultural_stock.market_goods_imports < agricultural_stock_market_goods_sell_orders_ratio
			not = {

			}
		}
		remove_banned_goods = g:agricultural_stock
	}
	######################
	######################
	#mining_stock
	if = {
		limit = { #tech à ajouter ici permet de délock l'import de pf étrangé au dela de 25%
			market.mg:mining_stock.market_goods_imports > mining_stock_market_goods_sell_orders_ratio
		}
		add_banned_goods = g:mining_stock
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			market.mg:mining_stock.market_goods_imports < mining_stock_market_goods_sell_orders_ratio
			not = {
				has_modifier = speculative_share_modifier_1
			}
		}
		remove_banned_goods = g:mining_stock
	}
	######################
	######################
	#railroad_stock
	if = {
		limit = { #tech à ajouter ici permet de délock l'import de pf étrangé au dela de 25%
			market.mg:railroad_stock.market_goods_imports > railroad_stock_market_goods_sell_orders_ratio
		}
		add_banned_goods = g:railroad_stock
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			market.mg:railroad_stock.market_goods_imports < railroad_stock_market_goods_sell_orders_ratio
			not = {
				has_modifier = speculative_share_modifier_1
			}
		}
		remove_banned_goods = g:railroad_stock
	}
	######################

	#####################################################################################################################################################

	if = { #country_already_financial_center
		limit = {
			building_financial_centre_any = yes
			not = {
				var:country_already_financial_center = 1
			}
		}
		set_variable  = {
			name = country_already_financial_center
			value = 1
		}
	}
}
remove_country_building_modifier = { #country
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_modifier = has_financial_center
			NOT = {
				any_scope_state = {
					building_financial_centre_any = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = { limit = { has_modifier = has_financial_center } remove_modifier = has_financial_center }
	}
}
destroy_building_not_allowed = {
	every_scope_state = {
        if = {
            limit = {
                and = {
                    has_building = building_financial_centre
                    NOT = {
                        has_modifier = financial_center_place
                    }
                }
            }
            remove_building = building_financial_centre
        }
	}
}

financial_center_modifier_fixed_var = {
	set_variable = {
		name = base_demande_bond_fix
		value = base_demande_bond
	}
	set_variable = {
		name = base_demande_manufacture_stock_fix
		value = base_demande_manufacture_stock
	}
	set_variable = {
		name = base_demande_agricultural_stock_fix
		value = base_demande_agricultural_stock
	}
	set_variable = {
		name = base_demande_mining_stock_fix
		value = base_demande_mining_stock
	}
	set_variable = {
		name = base_demande_railroad_stock_fix
		value = base_demande_railroad_stock
	}
}

initialize_historic_macro_facilities_bc = { #history
	# the technologies -- in the countries' history (history/countries/ld_start_technologies.txt), the engine's law
	# check sees them there

	$CTRL_BANK_SITE$ = {
		owner = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = has_central_bank
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = central_bank_location
				value = $CTRL_BANK_SITE$
			}
			# the CB's size (Д.R9б.1; E&F: its building's levels)
			set_variable = { name = zz_ef_cb_size value = $CB_SIZE$ }
		}
	}
	central_bank_modifier = yes
}

#on_action

macro_facilities_on_action_bc = {
	if = {
		limit = { ### check to make sure we're ready and stable enough
			macro_facilities_on_action_bc_limit = yes
		}
		macro_facilities_bc = yes
	}
}
# the CB with the technology (Д.R9б.1): the modifier has_central_bank, its size by GDP -- E&F's building in the capital
# grown by a switch over var:gdp_view (5 levels a 5 million of GDP)
macro_facilities_bc = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_technology_researched = central_banking }
		zz_ef_cb_set = yes
	}

	law_no_monetary_system_with_tech_currency_standards = yes #pour avoir le law_fiat_standard si a le law_no_monetary_system
	central_bank_modifier = yes
	introduction_new_currency = yes
	#utile pour les pays qui vienne d'avoir la tech ou si il y a un probleme de bc de loi ou de varriable
	#doit etre proteégé par law_fiat_standart donc toujour à mettre juste avant cet effect si on veux reset la currency
	central_bank_modifier = yes
	central_bank_production_methods = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			market_owner_is_root = no
			market_owner_is_root_univ_with_buiding = no #na pas la configuration deja sujet actif evite les répétition
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			zz_ef_exchange_std = yes
			#securisé avec un trigger qui est invalide si la pm no subjet currency est selectionné
		}
		central_bank_production_methods_3 = yes
	}
	#money_value_target_modification = yes #probleme de set de la currency target
}

#reparation
#####################################################################################################################################################
has_tech_central_bank_but_no_building_central_bank = {
	### redundancy: treated in event central_bank_event.1
	if = {
		limit = { exists = capital }
		capital = {
			save_scope_as = central_bank_site
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { ### check to make sure we're ready and stable enough
			macro_facilities_on_action_bc_limit = yes
			not = { #vérifit si le modifier n'existe pas -> pas sure
				has_modifier = has_central_bank
			}
		}
		macro_facilities_on_action_bc = yes
	}
}
has_currency_law_but_no_building_central_bank = {
	### redundancy: treated in event central_bank_event.1
	if = {
		limit = { exists = capital }
		capital = {
			save_scope_as = central_bank_site
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { ### check to make sure we're ready and stable enough
			has_technology_researched = central_banking
			zz_ef_cb_law_has_cb = yes # the CB by law (Д.R4а.3)
			is_revolutionary =  no
			is_invalid_country = no
			or = {
				is_valid_country = yes
				country_already_central_bank = yes
				var:gdp_view >= 1
				is_player = yes
				#pas besoin de protection japon pck que les pays ayant deja eu une BC ici pêuvet
			}
			not  = {
				has_modifier = has_central_bank
			}
			or = {
				var:zz_ef_cur ?= flag:dinar
				var:zz_ef_cur ?= flag:dinar_algerian_dinar
				var:zz_ef_cur ?= flag:dinar_iraqi_dinar

#financial_crash
financial_crash_destroy_financial_center = {
	every_scope_state = {
		limit = { building_financial_centre_any = yes }
		zz_ef_fc_remove = yes
	}
}

#economic_crisis
# the CB comes out of an economic crisis at the smallest size (Д.R9б.1; E&F: its building razed and rebuilt at 5 levels)
economic_crisis_destroy_central_bank = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_modifier = has_central_bank }
		set_variable = { name = zz_ef_cb_size value = zz_ef_cb_size_step }
	}
}

#introduction_new_currency

###########################################################################################################################################################################################
###########################################################################################################################################################################################
###########################################################################################################################################################################################
###########################################################################################################################################################################################

# the country's currency: zz_ef_cur_intro (scripted_effects/ld_currency_intro.txt, Д.R8в.7)
introduction_new_currency = {
	#objectif de cet effect doit etre de réinitialiser à 100% la currency d'un pays comme si il venais d'avoir la tech
	#ce qui n'ai pas le cas car les pret étranger ne sont pas reset
	#doit utiliser le activate_law = law_type:law_no_market_liquidity avant d'appler l'effet si on veut un reset

	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = capital
			zz_ef_cb_law_has_cb = yes # the CB by law (Д.R4а.3)
			not = {
				has_modifier = has_central_bank
			}
		}
		zz_ef_cb_set = yes
		central_bank_modifier = yes
		central_bank_production_methods = yes
	}

	zz_ef_cur_intro = yes
}
